The kid really loved being able to put the bear and sit out outside with the bear in the skeleton. It’s on the water the boats. The food was excellent and I will definitely be back. Price is definitely not as expensive as the other waterfront properties. Can’t wait to come back next year
PS the baguettes… Donut holes with dips was the absolute best I’ve ever had. They were very light, crunchy and flaky. Not like a deep-fried, thick donut, but nice and light and flaky.

rating: 5
review_text: This was an absolutely wonderful dining experience!
We had a server, Ricardo, who had one of the friendliest and kindest smiles in all of south Alabama. His service was top tier and should be the standard of measure.
The food was phenomenal. It came out hot and fresh and very quick to the table from the time of order.
We had the blackened chicken tots, and the shrimp and crawfish nachos, and the grouper nuggets. The food was the best we've had all week! The grouper was fresh and delicious. I highly recommend it if you like fish!
Their Bushwackers are as good as any that we've tried.
This is by far one of the best spots in the Gulf Shores/ Orange Beach area.

id: 0ae8b34e-ddd5-41d0-affa-82b079289be8
entity_slug: oso-at-bear-point-harbor-orange-beach
reviewer_name: Daneen England
rating: 5
body: Definitely one of the better restaurants that we’ve been to. We had grilled snapper as well as blackened grouper and black and shrimp. Also the bang bang shrimp. The Asian slow coleslaw was fantastic. Hush puppies were spot on. The service was top-notch. The restaurant is super cute. Lots of little chickens running around outside. The kid really loved being able to put the bear and sit out outside with the bear in the skeleton. It’s on the water the boats. The food was excellent and I will definitely be back. Price is definitely not as expensive as the other waterfront properties. Can’t wait to come back next year
PS the baguettes… Donut holes with dips was the absolute best I’ve ever had. They were very light, crunchy and flaky. Not like a deep-fried, thick donut, but nice and light and flaky.
The two alcoholic drinks were awesome and very well made

The live music was great, as well. Highly recommend.

rating: 5
body: Came down to Oso on Bear Point today. Cool-laid back local vibe. Island time for sure. With great service. Has Happy Hour Monday-Friday from 2-6pm. $1.50 Busch Lights and cocktails. Bartenders are very friendly and attentive. They also have live music every night after 6pm. Outside. Inside-outside seating, patio seating outside inside, upstairs seating. This place is huge! Probably the coolest menu I’ve seen on the island yet! Menu is decently priced with awesome specials. Chef Ashton came out and welcomed me in personally today. What a pleasure!

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: 88963b1e-8b40-41d2-9df0-802d9f6b5433
entity_slug: oso-at-bear-point-harbor-orange-beach
reviewer_name: John Jones
rating: 5
body: This was an absolutely wonderful dining experience!
We had a server, Ricardo, who had one of the friendliest and kindest smiles in all of south Alabama. His service was top tier and should be the standard of measure.
The food was phenomenal. It came out hot and fresh and very quick to the table from the time of order.
We had the blackened chicken tots, and the shrimp and crawfish nachos, and the grouper nuggets. The food was the best we've had all week! The grouper was fresh and delicious. I highly recommend it if you like fish!
Their Bushwackers are as good as any that we've tried.
This is by far one of the best spots in the Gulf Shores/ Orange Beach area.
